    25→class SyncAlertService:
    26→    """Synchronous service for processing alerts and creating incidents.
    27→
    28→    For use in Celery worker tasks that run synchronously.
    29→    Notifications are queued as separate Celery tasks rather than sent inline.
    30→    """
    31→
    32→    def __init__(self, db: Session):
    33→        self.db = db
    34→
    35→    def trigger_alert(
    36→        self,
    37→        trigger_type: TriggerType,
    38→        severity: Severity,
    39→        title: str,
    40→        message: str,
    41→        resource_type: Optional[str] = None,
    42→        resource_id: Optional[str] = None,
    43→        details: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
    44→    ) -> List[Incident]:
    45→        """Trigger an alert based on an event.
    46→
    47→        This method:
    48→        1. Finds matching NotificationRules for this trigger_type
    49→        2. Checks cooldown (skip if recently triggered)
    50→        3. Creates Incident for each matching rule
    51→        4. Queues notifications to be sent (via Celery task)
    52→
    53→        Args:
    54→            trigger_type: The type of event that triggered this alert
    55→            severity: The severity level of the alert
    56→            title: Alert title
    57→            message: Alert description/message
    58→            resource_type: Optional resource type (e.g., "uptime_monitor", "budget")
    59→            resource_id: Optional resource identifier
    60→            details: Additional context details
    61→
    62→        Returns:
    63→            List of created Incident objects
    64→        """
    65→        created_incidents: List[Incident] = []
    66→
    67→        # Find matching notification rules
    68→        matching_rules = self._find_matching_rules(
    69→            trigger_type=trigger_type,
    70→            severity=severity,
    71→            resource_type=resource_type,
    72→            resource_id=resource_id,
    73→        )
    74→
    75→        if not matching_rules:
    76→            logger.info(
    77→                f"No matching notification rules for trigger_type={trigger_type.value}, "
    78→                f"severity={severity.value}"
    79→            )
    80→            # Still create an incident even without matching rules for tracking
    81→            incident = self._create_incident(
    82→                notification_rule_id=None,
    83→                trigger_type=trigger_type.value,
    84→                severity=severity.value,
    85→                title=title,
    86→                message=message,
    87→                resource_type=resource_type,
    88→                resource_id=resource_id,
    89→                details=details,
    90→            )
    91→            created_incidents.append(incident)
    92→
    93→            # CRITICAL: Commit incident FIRST before best-effort operations
    94→            # This ensures the incident is persisted even if downstream operations fail
    95→            self.db.commit()
    96→
    97→            # Best-effort: dispatch diagnostic tasks for infra incidents
    98→            self._dispatch_diagnostic_tasks(created_incidents)
    99→
   100→            # Best-effort operations - failures won't rollback the incident
   101→            self._do_best_effort_notifications(
   102→                incident=incident,
   103→                trigger_type=trigger_type,
   104→                title=title,
   105→                message=message,
   106→                severity=severity.value,
   107→            )
   108→
   109→            return created_incidents
   110→
   111→        for rule in matching_rules:
   112→            # Check cooldown
   113→            if not self._check_cooldown(rule):
   114→                logger.info(
   115→                    f"Skipping rule {rule.id} ({rule.name}) due to cooldown"
   116→                )
   117→                continue
   118→
   119→            # Create incident
   120→            incident = self._create_incident(
   121→                notification_rule_id=rule.id,
   122→                trigger_type=trigger_type.value,
   123→                severity=severity.value,
   124→                title=title,
   125→                message=message,
   126→                resource_type=resource_type,
   127→                resource_id=resource_id,
   128→                details=details,
   129→            )
   130→            created_incidents.append(incident)
   131→
   132→            # Update rule's last_triggered and trigger_count
   133→            rule.last_triggered = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   134→            rule.trigger_count = (rule.trigger_count or 0) + 1
   135→
   136→            # Queue notifications to configured channels
   137→            self._queue_notifications(
   138→                rule=rule,
   139→                title=title,
   140→                message=message,
   141→                severity=severity.value,
   142→                details=details,
   143→            )
   144→
   145→        # CRITICAL: Commit incidents and rule updates FIRST
   146→        # This ensures incidents are persisted even if downstream operations fail
   147→        self.db.commit()
   148→
   149→        logger.info(
   150→            f"Created {len(created_incidents)} incident(s) for "
   151→            f"trigger_type={trigger_type.value}"
   152→        )
   153→
   154→        # Best-effort: dispatch diagnostic tasks for infra incidents
   155→        self._dispatch_diagnostic_tasks(created_incidents)
   156→
   157→        # Best-effort operations - failures won't rollback the incidents
   158→        for incident in created_incidents:
   159→            self._do_best_effort_notifications(
   160→                incident=incident,
   161→                trigger_type=trigger_type,
   162→                title=title,
   163→                message=message,
   164→                severity=severity.value,
   165→            )
   166→
   167→        return created_incidents
   168→
   169→    def _dispatch_diagnostic_tasks(self, incidents: List[Incident]):
   170→        """Best-effort: queue diagnostic tasks for infrastructure incidents.
   171→
   172→        Only dispatches for host/docker resource types where resource_id
   173→        is the OTel agent ID.
   174→        """
   175→        for incident in incidents:
   176→            try:
   177→                if incident.resource_type in ("host", "docker") and incident.resource_id:
   178→                    from workers.tasks.diagnostic import dispatch_diagnostic_task
   179→                    dispatch_diagnostic_task.delay(incident_id=incident.id)
   180→            except Exception as e:
   181→                logger.warning(
   182→                    f"Failed to queue diagnostic task for incident {incident.id}: {e}"
   183→                )
   184→
   185→    def send_recovery_notifications(
   186→        self,
   187→        trigger_type: TriggerType,
   188→        severity: Severity,
   189→        title: str,
   190→        message: str,
   191→        resource_type: Optional[str] = None,
   192→        resource_id: Optional[str] = None,
   193→        details: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   194→    ) -> bool:
   195→        """Send recovery notifications without creating incidents.
   196→
   197→        Used for MONITOR_UP events: sends alerts through configured notification
   198→        channels (email, Slack, etc.) but does NOT create an incident record.
   199→        This keeps the incident list clean (one incident per outage, not two).
   200→
   201→        Args:
   202→            trigger_type: The recovery trigger type (e.g., MONITOR_UP)
   203→            severity: The severity level
   204→            title: Notification title
   205→            message: Notification message
   206→            resource_type: Optional resource type
   207→            resource_id: Optional resource identifier
   208→            details: Additional context details
   209→
   210→        Returns:
   211→            True if any notifications were sent, False otherwise
   212→        """
   213→        matching_rules = self._find_matching_rules(
   214→            trigger_type=trigger_type,
   215→            severity=severity,
   216→            resource_type=resource_type,
   217→            resource_id=resource_id,
   218→        )
   219→
   220→        if not matching_rules:
   221→            logger.info(
   222→                f"No matching notification rules for recovery "
   223→                f"trigger_type={trigger_type.value}, severity={severity.value}"
   224→            )
   225→            return False
   226→
   227→        notifications_sent = False
   228→        for rule in matching_rules:
   229→            if not self._check_cooldown(rule):
   230→                logger.info(
   231→                    f"Skipping recovery rule {rule.id} ({rule.name}) due to cooldown"
   232→                )
   233→                continue
   234→
   235→            # Queue notifications to configured channels
   236→            self._queue_notifications(
   237→                rule=rule,
   238→                title=title,
   239→                message=message,
   240→                severity=severity.value,
   241→                details=details,
   242→            )
   243→
   244→            # Update rule's last_triggered and trigger_count
   245→            rule.last_triggered = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   246→            rule.trigger_count = (rule.trigger_count or 0) + 1
   247→            notifications_sent = True
   248→
   249→        if notifications_sent:
   250→            self.db.commit()
   251→            logger.info(
   252→                f"Sent recovery notifications for trigger_type={trigger_type.value}"
   253→            )
   254→
   255→        return notifications_sent
   256→
   257→    def _find_matching_rules(
   258→        self,
   259→        trigger_type: TriggerType,
   260→        severity: Severity,
   261→        resource_type: Optional[str] = None,
   262→        resource_id: Optional[str] = None,
   263→    ) -> List[NotificationRule]:
   264→        """Find notification rules that match the given event."""
   265→        # Base query: active rules with matching trigger type
   266→        rules = self.db.execute(
   267→            select(NotificationRule).where(
   268→                and_(
   269→                    NotificationRule.is_active == True,
   270→                    NotificationRule.trigger_type == trigger_type,
   271→                )
   272→            )
   273→        ).scalars().all()
   274→
   275→        # Filter by severity and conditions
   276→        matching_rules = []
   277→        for rule in rules:
   278→            # Check severity filter
   279→            if rule.severity_filter:
   280→                if severity.value not in rule.severity_filter:
   281→                    continue
   282→
   283→            # Check conditions
   284→            if rule.conditions:
   285→                if not self._check_conditions(
   286→                    rule.conditions, resource_type, resource_id
   287→                ):
   288→                    continue
   289→
   290→            matching_rules.append(rule)
   291→
   292→        return matching_rules
   293→
   294→    def _check_conditions(
   295→        self,
   296→        conditions: Dict[str, Any],
   297→        resource_type: Optional[str],
   298→        resource_id: Optional[str],
   299→    ) -> bool:
   300→        """Check if event matches rule conditions."""
   301→        # Check resource_types filter
   302→        if "resource_types" in conditions and conditions["resource_types"]:
   303→            if resource_type not in conditions["resource_types"]:
   304→                return False
   305→
   306→        # Check monitor_ids filter
   307→        if "monitor_ids" in conditions and conditions["monitor_ids"]:
   308→            if resource_type == "uptime_monitor" and resource_id:
   309→                try:
   310→                    monitor_id = int(resource_id)
   311→                    if monitor_id not in conditions["monitor_ids"]:
   312→                        return False
   313→                except (ValueError, TypeError):
   314→                    return False
   315→
   316→        return True
   317→
   318→    def _check_cooldown(self, rule: NotificationRule) -> bool:
   319→        """Check if the rule's cooldown period has elapsed.
   320→
   321→        Returns True if alert should proceed (cooldown elapsed or disabled).
   322→        Returns False if still in cooldown period.
   323→        """
   324→        # Cooldown of 0 means disabled - always allow
   325→        cooldown_minutes = rule.cooldown_minutes if rule.cooldown_minutes is not None else 0
   326→        if cooldown_minutes == 0:
   327→            return True
   328→
   329→        if not rule.last_triggered:
   330→            return True
   331→
   332→        cooldown_delta = timedelta(minutes=cooldown_minutes)
   333→        cooldown_end = rule.last_triggered + cooldown_delta
   334→
   335→        return datetime.now(timezone.utc) >= cooldown_end
   336→
   337→    def _reset_cooldown_for_incident(self, incident: Incident) -> None:
   338→        """Reset the cooldown for the notification rule associated with an incident.
   339→
   340→        When an incident is resolved (manually or auto), the cooldown should be reset
   341→        so that if the same issue occurs again, a new alert will be triggered immediately.
   342→        This prevents recurring issues from being masked by the cooldown period.
   343→
   344→        Args:
   345→            incident: The resolved incident whose rule's cooldown should be reset.
   346→        """
   347→        if not incident.notification_rule_id:
   348→            return
   349→
   350→        rule = self.db.execute(
   351→            select(NotificationRule).where(
   352→                NotificationRule.id == incident.notification_rule_id
   353→            )
   354→        ).scalar_one_or_none()
   355→
   356→        if rule and rule.last_triggered:
   357→            rule.last_triggered = None
   358→            logger.info(
   359→                f"Reset cooldown for notification rule {rule.id} ({rule.name}) "
   360→                f"after resolving incident {incident.id}"
   361→            )
   362→
   363→    def _create_incident(
   364→        self,
   365→        notification_rule_id: Optional[int],
   366→        trigger_type: str,
   367→        severity: str,
   368→        title: str,
   369→        message: str,
   370→        resource_type: Optional[str],
   371→        resource_id: Optional[str],
   372→        details: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
   373→    ) -> Incident:
   374→        """Create a new incident record."""
   375→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   376→        incident = Incident(
   377→            notification_rule_id=notification_rule_id,
   378→            title=title,
   379→            description=message,
   380→            status=IncidentStatus.OPEN,
   381→            severity=severity,
   382→            trigger_type=trigger_type,
   383→            resource_type=resource_type,
   384→            resource_id=resource_id,
   385→            details=details,
   386→            started_at=now,
   387→        )
   388→
   389→        self.db.add(incident)
   390→        self.db.flush()  # Get the ID without committing
   391→
   392→        logger.info(f"Created incident {incident.id}: {title}")
   393→
   394→        return incident
   395→
   396→    def _queue_notifications(
   397→        self,
   398→        rule: NotificationRule,
   399→        title: str,
   400→        message: str,
   401→        severity: str,
   402→        details: Optional[Dict[str, Any]],
   403→    ) -> None:
   404→        """Queue notifications to all channels configured in the rule."""
   405→        if not rule.channel_ids:
   406→            logger.warning(f"Rule {rule.id} has no channel_ids configured")
   407→            return
   408→
   409→        # Import here to avoid circular imports
   410→        from workers.tasks.notifications import send_notification_task
   411→
   412→        for channel_id in rule.channel_ids:
   413→            try:
   414→                # Verify channel exists and is active
   415→                channel = self.db.execute(
   416→                    select(NotificationChannel).where(
   417→                        NotificationChannel.id == channel_id
   418→                    )
   419→                ).scalar_one_or_none()
   420→
   421→                if not channel:
   422→                    logger.warning(
   423→                        f"Channel {channel_id} not found for rule {rule.id}"
   424→                    )
   425→                    continue
   426→
   427→                if not channel.is_active:
   428→                    logger.info(
   429→                        f"Skipping inactive channel {channel_id} for rule {rule.id}"
   430→                    )
   431→                    continue
   432→
   433→                # Build notification details
   434→                notification_details = details.copy() if details else {}
   435→                if rule.include_details:
   436→                    notification_details["rule_name"] = rule.name
   437→                    notification_details["rule_id"] = rule.id
   438→
   439→                # Queue the notification as a Celery task
   440→                send_notification_task.delay(
   441→                    channel_id=channel_id,
   442→                    title=title,
   443→                    message=message,
   444→                    severity=severity,
   445→                    details=notification_details,
   446→                )
   447→
   448→                logger.info(
   449→                    f"Queued notification for channel {channel_id} "
   450→                    f"({channel.channel_type.value})"
   451→                )
   452→
   453→            except Exception as e:
   454→                logger.error(
   455→                    f"Error queueing notification for channel {channel_id}: {e}"
   456→                )
   457→
   458→    def auto_resolve_incidents(
   459→        self,
   460→        trigger_type: TriggerType,
   461→        resource_type: str,
   462→        resource_id: str,
   463→        send_notifications: bool = True,
   464→    ) -> List[Incident]:
   465→        """Auto-resolve open incidents when a recovery event occurs.
   466→
   467→        When MONITOR_UP is triggered, this resolves both MONITOR_DOWN and
   468→        MONITOR_DEGRADED incidents for the same resource.
   469→
   470→        Args:
   471→            trigger_type: The recovery trigger type
   472→            resource_type: The resource type (e.g., "uptime_monitor")
   473→            resource_id: The resource identifier
   474→            send_notifications: Whether to send resolution notifications (default True)
   475→        """
   476→        # Map recovery triggers to their incident triggers
   477→        # MONITOR_UP should resolve both DOWN and DEGRADED incidents
   478→        recovery_map = {
   479→            TriggerType.MONITOR_UP: [TriggerType.MONITOR_DOWN, TriggerType.MONITOR_DEGRADED],
   480→        }
   481→
   482→        incident_triggers = recovery_map.get(trigger_type)
   483→        if not incident_triggers:
   484→            return []
   485→
   486→        # Find open incidents for this resource (matching any of the incident triggers)
   487→        incident_trigger_values = [t.value for t in incident_triggers]
   488→        incidents = self.db.execute(
   489→            select(Incident).where(
   490→                and_(
   491→                    Incident.status.in_([IncidentStatus.OPEN, IncidentStatus.ACKNOWLEDGED]),
   492→                    Incident.trigger_type.in_(incident_trigger_values),
   493→                    Incident.resource_type == resource_type,
   494→                    Incident.resource_id == resource_id,
   495→                )
   496→            )
   497→        ).scalars().all()
   498→
   499→        resolved_incidents = []
   500→        for incident in incidents:
   501→            incident.status = IncidentStatus.RESOLVED
   502→            incident.resolved_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   503→            incident.notes = (
   504→                f"{incident.notes or ''}\n\nAuto-resolved: Recovery detected"
   505→            ).strip()
   506→            resolved_incidents.append(incident)
   507→            logger.info(f"Auto-resolved incident {incident.id}: {incident.title}")
   508→
   509→            # Reset cooldown so future alerts can trigger immediately
   510→            self._reset_cooldown_for_incident(incident)
   511→
   512→            # Send resolution notification (bypasses cooldown)
   513→            if send_notifications:
   514→                self._send_resolution_notification(
   515→                    incident=incident,
   516→                    resolved_by=None,  # Auto-resolved
   517→                    resolution_notes="Recovery detected automatically",
   518→                )
   519→
   520→        if resolved_incidents:
   521→            # CRITICAL: Commit resolution status AND cooldown reset FIRST
   522→            self.db.commit()
   523→
   524→            # Best-effort: Create in-app notifications and publish real-time events
   525→            for incident in resolved_incidents:
   526→                try:
   527→                    self._create_in_app_notifications(
   528→                        incident=incident,
   529→                        notification_type=NotificationType.INCIDENT_RESOLVED,
   530→                        title=f"Resolved: {incident.title}",
   531→                        message="Incident has been auto-resolved: Recovery detected",
   532→                        severity="info",
   533→                    )
   534→                    self.db.commit()
   535→                except Exception as e:
   536→                    logger.warning(f"In-app notification for resolution failed: {e}")
   537→                    try:
   538→                        self.db.rollback()
   539→                    except Exception:
   540→                        pass
   541→
   542→                try:
   543→                    self._publish_realtime_event(
   544→                        event_type="incident_resolved",
   545→                        incident=incident,
   546→                        title=f"Resolved: {incident.title}",
   547→                        severity="info",
   548→                        trigger_type=incident.trigger_type,
   549→                    )
   550→                except Exception as e:
   551→                    logger.warning(f"Realtime event for resolution failed: {e}")
   552→                # Note: No push notifications for resolutions (info severity, not urgent)
   553→
   554→        return resolved_incidents
   555→
   556→    def _send_resolution_notification(
   557→        self,
   558→        incident: Incident,
   559→        resolved_by: Optional[str] = None,
   560→        resolution_notes: Optional[str] = None,
   561→    ) -> None:
   562→        """Send notification when an incident is resolved.
   563→
   564→        Resolution notifications bypass cooldown since users should always
   565→        be informed when issues are fixed.
   566→
   567→        Args:
   568→            incident: The resolved incident
   569→            resolved_by: Name of the user who resolved (None for auto-resolve)
   570→            resolution_notes: Optional resolution notes
   571→        """
   572→        # If incident has no notification rule, we can't send notification
   573→        if not incident.notification_rule_id:
   574→            logger.info(
   575→                f"Incident {incident.id} has no notification rule, skipping resolution notification"
   576→            )
   577→            return
   578→
   579→        # Get the notification rule
   580→        rule = self.db.execute(
   581→            select(NotificationRule).where(
   582→                NotificationRule.id == incident.notification_rule_id
   583→            )
   584→        ).scalar_one_or_none()
   585→
   586→        if not rule:
   587→            logger.warning(
   588→                f"Notification rule {incident.notification_rule_id} not found for incident {incident.id}"
   589→            )
   590→            return
   591→
   592→        if not rule.is_active:
   593→            logger.info(
   594→                f"Notification rule {rule.id} is inactive, skipping resolution notification"
   595→            )
   596→            return
   597→
   598→        # Build resolution message
   599→        resolver = resolved_by or "System (auto-resolved)"
   600→        title = f"✅ Resolved: {incident.title}"
   601→        message = f"Incident has been resolved by {resolver}."
   602→        if resolution_notes:
   603→            message += f"\n\nNotes: {resolution_notes}"
   604→
   605→        # Calculate incident duration
   606→        if incident.started_at and incident.resolved_at:
   607→            duration = incident.resolved_at - incident.started_at
   608→            hours, remainder = divmod(int(duration.total_seconds()), 3600)
   609→            minutes, seconds = divmod(remainder, 60)
   610→            if hours > 0:
   611→                duration_str = f"{hours}h {minutes}m"
   612→            elif minutes > 0:
   613→                duration_str = f"{minutes}m {seconds}s"
   614→            else:
   615→                duration_str = f"{seconds}s"
   616→            message += f"\n\nDuration: {duration_str}"
   617→
   618→        details = {
   619→            "incident_id": incident.id,
   620→            "original_severity": incident.severity,
   621→            "original_trigger_type": incident.trigger_type,
   622→            "resource_type": incident.resource_type,
   623→            "resource_id": incident.resource_id,
   624→            "resolved_by": resolver,
   625→            "resolution_type": "auto" if resolved_by is None else "manual",
   626→        }
   627→        if incident.details:
   628→            details["original_details"] = incident.details
   629→
   630→        # Queue notifications (bypass cooldown for resolutions)
   631→        self._queue_notifications(
   632→            rule=rule,
   633→            title=title,
   634→            message=message,
   635→            severity="info",  # Resolutions are always info severity
   636→            details=details,
   637→        )
   638→
   639→        logger.info(
   640→            f"Queued resolution notification for incident {incident.id} via rule {rule.id}"
   641→        )
   642→
   643→    def _do_best_effort_notifications(
   644→        self,
   645→        incident: Incident,
   646→        trigger_type: TriggerType,
   647→        title: str,
   648→        message: str,
   649→        severity: str,
   650→    ) -> None:
   651→        """Perform best-effort notification operations after incident is committed.
   652→
   653→        These operations can fail without affecting the incident persistence.
   654→        Each operation is wrapped in its own try-catch to isolate failures.
   655→        """
   656→        # 1. Create in-app notifications (has its own commit)
   657→        try:
   658→            self._create_in_app_notifications(
   659→                incident=incident,
   660→                notification_type=self._map_trigger_to_notification_type(trigger_type),
   661→                title=title,
   662→                message=message,
   663→                severity=severity,
   664→            )
   665→            self.db.commit()
   666→        except Exception as e:
   667→            logger.warning(f"In-app notifications failed (non-critical): {e}")
   668→            try:
   669→                self.db.rollback()
   670→            except Exception:
   671→                pass  # Session may already be in a bad state
   672→
   673→        # 2. Publish real-time event (non-DB operation)
   674→        try:
   675→            self._publish_realtime_event(
   676→                event_type="incident_created",
   677→                incident=incident,
   678→                title=title,
   679→                severity=severity,
   680→                trigger_type=trigger_type.value,
   681→            )
   682→        except Exception as e:
   683→            logger.warning(f"Realtime event failed (non-critical): {e}")
   684→
   685→        # 3. Queue push notifications (Celery task)
   686→        try:
   687→            self._queue_push_notifications(
   688→                title=title,
   689→                body=message,
   690→                url=f"/incidents?open={incident.id}",
   691→                severity=severity,
   692→                trigger_type=trigger_type.value,
   693→            )
   694→        except Exception as e:
   695→            logger.warning(f"Push notification queueing failed (non-critical): {e}")
   696→
   697→    def _map_trigger_to_notification_type(self, trigger_type: TriggerType) -> NotificationType:
   698→        """Map TriggerType to NotificationType for in-app notifications."""
   699→        mapping = {
   700→            TriggerType.MONITOR_DOWN: NotificationType.MONITOR_DOWN,
   701→            TriggerType.MONITOR_UP: NotificationType.MONITOR_UP,
   702→            TriggerType.MONITOR_DEGRADED: NotificationType.MONITOR_DOWN,  # Use monitor_down for degraded too
   703→            TriggerType.SSL_EXPIRING: NotificationType.SSL_EXPIRING,
   704→            TriggerType.SSL_EXPIRED: NotificationType.SSL_EXPIRED,
   705→            TriggerType.BUDGET_THRESHOLD: NotificationType.BUDGET_THRESHOLD,
   706→            TriggerType.BUDGET_EXCEEDED: NotificationType.BUDGET_EXCEEDED,
   707→            TriggerType.HOST_METRIC_THRESHOLD: NotificationType.HOST_ALERT,
   708→            TriggerType.CONTAINER_METRIC_THRESHOLD: NotificationType.CONTAINER_ALERT,
   709→            TriggerType.CLOUDWATCH_METRIC_THRESHOLD: NotificationType.CLOUDWATCH_ALERT,
   710→        }
   711→        return mapping.get(trigger_type, NotificationType.INCIDENT_CREATED)
   712→
   713→    def _create_in_app_notifications(
   714→        self,
   715→        incident: Incident,
   716→        notification_type: NotificationType,
   717→        title: str,
   718→        message: str,
   719→        severity: str,
   720→    ) -> None:
   721→        """Create in-app notification records for all active users.
   722→
   723→        Respects each user's NotificationPreference (severity and trigger type filters).
   724→        """
   725→        try:
   726→            # Get all active users
   727→            result = self.db.execute(
   728→                select(User).where(User.is_active == True)
   729→            )
   730→            users = list(result.scalars().all())
   731→
   732→            # Get all preferences in one query
   733→            pref_result = self.db.execute(
   734→                select(NotificationPreference)
   735→            )
   736→            prefs_by_user = {
   737→                p.user_id: p for p in pref_result.scalars().all()
   738→            }
   739→
   740→            for user in users:
   741→                pref = prefs_by_user.get(user.id)
   742→
   743→                # Check if user has in-app notifications disabled
   744→                if pref and not pref.in_app_enabled:
   745→                    continue
   746→
   747→                # Check severity filter
   748→                if pref and pref.severity_filter:
   749→                    if severity not in pref.severity_filter:
   750→                        continue
   751→
   752→                # Check trigger type filter
   753→                if pref and pref.trigger_types:
   754→                    if notification_type.value not in pref.trigger_types:
   755→                        continue
   756→
   757→                notification = InAppNotification(
   758→                    user_id=user.id,
   759→                    type=notification_type.value,  # Use .value to get lowercase string for postgres enum
   760→                    title=title,
   761→                    message=message,
   762→                    severity=severity,
   763→                    incident_id=incident.id,
   764→                    resource_type=incident.resource_type,
   765→                    resource_id=incident.resource_id,
   766→                    action_url=f"/incidents?open={incident.id}",
   767→                    details=incident.details,
   768→                )
   769→                self.db.add(notification)
   770→
   771→            logger.info(f"Created in-app notifications for {len(users)} users")
   772→
   773→        except Exception as e:
   774→            logger.error(f"Failed to create in-app notifications: {e}")
   775→
   776→    def _publish_realtime_event(
   777→        self,
   778→        event_type: str,
   779→        incident: Incident,
   780→        title: str,
   781→        severity: str,
   782→        trigger_type: str,
   783→    ) -> None:
   784→        """Publish a real-time event via Redis pub/sub for WebSocket delivery."""
   785→        try:
   786→            RealtimeService.publish_event_sync(
   787→                event_type=event_type,
   788→                data={
   789→                    "incident_id": incident.id,
   790→                    "title": title,
   791→                    "severity": severity,
   792→                    "trigger_type": trigger_type,
   793→                    "resource_type": incident.resource_type,
   794→                    "resource_id": incident.resource_id,
   795→                    "status": incident.status.value if hasattr(incident.status, 'value') else str(incident.status),
   796→                },
   797→                target_user_ids=None,  # Broadcast to all
   798→            )
   799→        except Exception as e:
   800→            logger.error(f"Failed to publish real-time event: {e}")
   801→
   802→    def _queue_push_notifications(
   803→        self,
   804→        title: str,
   805→        body: str,
   806→        url: str,
   807→        severity: str,
   808→        trigger_type: str,
   809→    ) -> None:
   810→        """Queue browser push notifications via Celery for eligible users."""
   811→        try:
   812→            from workers.tasks.push_notifications import send_push_notification_task
   813→
   814→            # Get users who have push enabled and match filters
   815→            now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   816→            current_time = now.time()
   817→
   818→            result = self.db.execute(
   819→                select(NotificationPreference).where(
   820→                    NotificationPreference.push_enabled == True
   821→                )
   822→            )
   823→            preferences = list(result.scalars().all())
   824→
   825→            eligible_user_ids = []
   826→            for pref in preferences:
   827→                # Check severity filter
   828→                if pref.severity_filter and severity not in pref.severity_filter:
   829→                    continue
   830→
   831→                # Check trigger type filter
   832→                if pref.trigger_types and trigger_type not in pref.trigger_types:
   833→                    continue
   834→
   835→                # Check quiet hours
   836→                if pref.quiet_hours_start and pref.quiet_hours_end:
   837→                    quiet_start = pref.quiet_hours_start
   838→                    quiet_end = pref.quiet_hours_end
   839→
   840→                    # Handle overnight quiet hours (e.g., 22:00 - 07:00)
   841→                    if quiet_start > quiet_end:
   842→                        # Quiet hours span midnight
   843→                        if current_time >= quiet_start or current_time <= quiet_end:
   844→                            continue
   845→                    else:
   846→                        # Normal quiet hours (e.g., 09:00 - 17:00)
   847→                        if quiet_start <= current_time <= quiet_end:
   848→                            continue
   849→
   850→                eligible_user_ids.append(pref.user_id)
   851→
   852→            if eligible_user_ids:
   853→                send_push_notification_task.delay(
   854→                    user_ids=eligible_user_ids,
   855→                    title=title,
   856→                    body=body,
   857→                    url=url,
   858→                    severity=severity,
   859→                )
   860→                logger.info(f"Queued push notifications for {len(eligible_user_ids)} users")
   861→            else:
   862→                logger.info("No eligible users for push notifications")
   863→
   864→        except Exception as e:
   865→            logger.error(f"Failed to queue push notifications: {e}")
   866→
   867→
   868→# Convenience function for use in worker tasks
   869→def trigger_alert_sync(
   870→    db: Session,
   871→    trigger_type: TriggerType,
   872→    severity: Severity,
   873→    title: str,
   874→    message: str,
   875→    resource_type: Optional[str] = None,
   876→    resource_id: Optional[str] = None,
   877→    details: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
   878→) -> List[Incident]:
   879→    """Convenience function to trigger an alert synchronously.
   880→
   881→    Usage in worker tasks:
   882→        from app.services.alert_service_sync import trigger_alert_sync
   883→        from app.models.notification_rule import TriggerType, Severity
   884→
   885→        with sync_db_session() as db:
   886→            trigger_alert_sync(
   887→                db=db,
   888→                trigger_type=TriggerType.MONITOR_DOWN,
   889→                severity=Severity.CRITICAL,
   890→                title=f"Monitor Down: {monitor.name}",
   891→                message=f"URL {monitor.url} is not responding",
   892→                resource_type="uptime_monitor",
   893→                resource_id=str(monitor.id),
   894→                details={"url": monitor.url, "error": error_message},
   895→            )
   896→    """
   897→    service = SyncAlertService(db)
   898→    return service.trigger_alert(
   899→        trigger_type=trigger_type,
   900→        severity=severity,
   901→        title=title,
   902→        message=message,
   903→        resource_type=resource_type,
   904→        resource_id=resource_id,
   905→        details=details,
   906→    )
   907→
   908→
   909→def send_resolution_notification_sync(
   910→    db: Session,
   911→    incident: Incident,
   912→    resolved_by: Optional[str] = None,
   913→    resolution_notes: Optional[str] = None,
   914→) -> None:
   915→    """Send resolution notification synchronously.
   916→
   917→    Use this when manually resolving incidents in synchronous code.
   918→
   919→    Args:
   920→        db: Database session
   921→        incident: The resolved incident
   922→        resolved_by: Name of the user who resolved (None for auto-resolve)
   923→        resolution_notes: Optional resolution notes
   924→    """
   925→    service = SyncAlertService(db)
   926→    service._send_resolution_notification(
   927→        incident=incident,
   928→        resolved_by=resolved_by,
   929→        resolution_notes=resolution_notes,
   930→    )
   931→

     1→import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
     2→import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
     3→import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
     4→import { io, Socket } from 'socket.io-client'
     5→import { useToast } from '@/components/Toast'
     6→
     7→interface WebSocketEvent {
     8→  incident_id: number
     9→  title: string
    10→  severity: string
    11→  trigger_type: string
    12→  resource_type?: string
    13→  resource_id?: string
    14→  status?: string
    15→}
    16→
    17→export function useWebSocket() {
    18→  const { data: session, status: sessionStatus } = useSession()
    19→  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
    20→  const toast = useToast()
    21→  const socketRef = useRef<Socket | null>(null)
    22→  const [isConnected, setIsConnected] = useState(false)
    23→
    24→  const invalidateNotificationQueries = useCallback(() => {
    25→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['notifications'] })
    26→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['unreadCount'] })
    27→  }, [queryClient])
    28→
    29→  const invalidateIncidentQueries = useCallback(() => {
    30→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['incidents'] })
    31→    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['incidentSummary'] })
    32→  }, [queryClient])
    33→
    34→  useEffect(() => {
    35→    if (sessionStatus !== 'authenticated' || !session?.accessToken) {
    36→      return
    37→    }
    38→
    39→    // In production, Socket.IO goes through nginx (same origin).
    40→    // In development (port 3000), Next.js rewrites don't support WebSocket
    41→    // upgrades, so we connect directly to the backend on port 8000.
    42→    const isDev = typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.location.port === '3000'
    43→    const socketUrl = isDev
    44→      ? `${window.location.protocol}//${window.location.hostname}:8000`
    45→      : undefined  // undefined = same origin (through nginx)
    46→
    47→    const socket = io(socketUrl, {
    48→      path: '/socket.io/',
    49→      auth: { token: session.accessToken },
    50→      transports: ['polling', 'websocket'],
    51→      reconnection: true,
    52→      reconnectionAttempts: Infinity,
    53→      reconnectionDelay: 1000,
    54→      reconnectionDelayMax: 30000,
    55→    })
    56→
    57→    socketRef.current = socket
    58→
    59→    socket.on('connect', () => {
    60→      setIsConnected(true)
    61→    })
    62→
    63→    socket.on('disconnect', () => {
    64→      setIsConnected(false)
    65→    })
    66→
    67→    socket.on('connected', () => {
    68→      // Server confirmed JWT authentication
    69→    })
    70→
    71→    socket.on('incident_created', (data: WebSocketEvent) => {
    72→      invalidateIncidentQueries()
    73→      invalidateNotificationQueries()
    74→
    75→      const severityToast = data.severity === 'critical' ? 'error' : data.severity === 'warning' ? 'warning' : 'info'
    76→      toast.showToast(severityToast, `New incident: ${data.title}`, 6000)
    77→    })
    78→
    79→    socket.on('incident_resolved', (data: WebSocketEvent) => {
    80→      invalidateIncidentQueries()
    81→      invalidateNotificationQueries()
    82→
    83→      toast.success(`Resolved: ${data.title}`, 5000)
    84→    })
    85→
    86→    socket.on('incident_acknowledged', (data: WebSocketEvent) => {
    87→      invalidateIncidentQueries()
    88→      invalidateNotificationQueries()
    89→    })
    90→
    91→    socket.on('notification', () => {
    92→      invalidateNotificationQueries()
    93→    })
    94→
    95→    socket.on('connect_error', () => {
    96→      setIsConnected(false)
    97→    })
    98→
    99→    return () => {
   100→      socket.disconnect()
   101→      socketRef.current = null
   102→      setIsConnected(false)
   103→    }
   104→  }, [session?.accessToken, sessionStatus, invalidateIncidentQueries, invalidateNotificationQueries, toast])
   105→
   106→  return { isConnected }
   107→}
   108→

     1→"""Real-time event service using Redis pub/sub.
     2→
     3→This service bridges business logic events to connected WebSocket clients.
     4→Events are published to a Redis channel and picked up by the Socket.IO
     5→server, which forwards them to connected users.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→import json
     9→import logging
    10→from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
    11→
    12→from app.core.config import settings
    13→
    14→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    15→
    16→EVENTS_CHANNEL = "bit:events"
    17→
    18→
    19→class RealtimeService:
    20→    """Publishes events to Redis pub/sub for WebSocket distribution."""
    21→
    22→    @staticmethod
    23→    async def publish_event(
    24→        event_type: str,
    25→        data: Dict[str, Any],
    26→        target_user_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None,
    27→    ) -> None:
    28→        """Publish an event to Redis for real-time distribution.
    29→
    30→        Args:
    31→            event_type: Event name (e.g. incident_created, notification)
    32→            data: JSON-serializable payload
    33→            target_user_ids: If None, broadcast to all; if list, only those users
    34→        """
    35→        try:
    36→            import redis.asyncio as aioredis
    37→
    38→            r = aioredis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL)
    39→            message = json.dumps({
    40→                "event": event_type,
    41→                "data": data,
    42→                "target_user_ids": target_user_ids,
    43→            })
    44→            await r.publish(EVENTS_CHANNEL, message)
    45→            await r.aclose()
    46→            logger.debug(f"Published event '{event_type}' to Redis")
    47→        except Exception as e:
    48→            logger.error(f"Failed to publish event '{event_type}': {e}")
    49→
    50→    @staticmethod
    51→    def publish_event_sync(
    52→        event_type: str,
    53→        data: Dict[str, Any],
    54→        target_user_ids: Optional[List[int]] = None,
    55→    ) -> None:
    56→        """Synchronous version for use in Celery workers.
    57→
    58→        Args:
    59→            event_type: Event name
    60→            data: JSON-serializable payload
    61→            target_user_ids: If None, broadcast to all; if list, only those users
    62→        """
    63→        try:
    64→            import redis
    65→
    66→            r = redis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL)
    67→            message = json.dumps({
    68→                "event": event_type,
    69→                "data": data,
    70→                "target_user_ids": target_user_ids,
    71→            })
    72→            r.publish(EVENTS_CHANNEL, message)
    73→            r.close()
    74→            logger.debug(f"Published event '{event_type}' to Redis (sync)")
    75→        except Exception as e:
    76→            logger.error(f"Failed to publish event '{event_type}' (sync): {e}")
    77→

     1→from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
     2→from fastapi.security import HTTPBearer, HTTPAuthorizationCredentials
     3→from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
     4→from sqlalchemy import select
     5→
     6→from app.core.database import get_db
     7→from app.core.security import decode_token
     8→from app.core.token_blacklist import is_token_blacklisted
     9→from app.models.user import User, UserRole
    10→
    11→security = HTTPBearer()
    12→
    13→
    14→async def get_current_user(
    15→    credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(security),
    16→    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    17→) -> User:
    18→    """Get the current authenticated user.
    19→
    20→    Requires full authentication including MFA verification (if enabled).
    21→    """
    22→    token = credentials.credentials
    23→    payload = decode_token(token)
    24→
    25→    if payload is None:
    26→        raise HTTPException(
    27→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    28→            detail="Invalid or expired token",
    29→        )
    30→
    31→    # Check if token has been revoked (logout, password change, etc.)
    32→    if await is_token_blacklisted(token):
    33→        raise HTTPException(
    34→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    35→            detail="Token has been revoked",
    36→        )
    37→
    38→    if payload.get("type") != "access":
    39→        raise HTTPException(
    40→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    41→            detail="Invalid token type",
    42→        )
    43→
    44→    # Check MFA verification status
    45→    # Tokens with mfa_verified=False are partial tokens pending MFA
    46→    if payload.get("mfa_verified") is False:
    47→        raise HTTPException(
    48→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    49→            detail="MFA verification required",
    50→            headers={"X-MFA-Required": "true"},
    51→        )
    52→
    53→    user_id = payload.get("sub")
    54→    if user_id is None:
    55→        raise HTTPException(
    56→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    57→            detail="Invalid token payload",
    58→        )
    59→
    60→    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == int(user_id)))
    61→    user = result.scalar_one_or_none()
    62→
    63→    if user is None:
    64→        raise HTTPException(
    65→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    66→            detail="User not found",
    67→        )
    68→
    69→    if not user.is_active:
    70→        raise HTTPException(
    71→            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
    72→            detail="User is inactive",
    73→        )
    74→
    75→    return user
    76→
    77→
    78→async def get_current_user_mfa_pending(
    79→    credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(security),
    80→    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    81→) -> User:
    82→    """Get current user from a partial token (MFA pending).
    83→
    84→    Used for the MFA verification endpoint. Accepts tokens with mfa_verified=False.
    85→    """
    86→    token = credentials.credentials
    87→    payload = decode_token(token)
    88→
    89→    if payload is None:
    90→        raise HTTPException(
    91→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    92→            detail="Invalid or expired token",
    93→        )
    94→
    95→    if payload.get("type") != "access":
    96→        raise HTTPException(
    97→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
    98→            detail="Invalid token type",
    99→        )
   100→
   101→    user_id = payload.get("sub")
   102→    if user_id is None:
   103→        raise HTTPException(
   104→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
   105→            detail="Invalid token payload",
   106→        )
   107→
   108→    result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.id == int(user_id)))
   109→    user = result.scalar_one_or_none()
   110→
   111→    if user is None:
   112→        raise HTTPException(
   113→            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,

   117→    if not user.is_active:

   124→
   125→
   126→async def get_current_superadmin(
   127→    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   128→) -> User:
   129→    """Require the current user to be a superadmin."""
   130→    if current_user.role != UserRole.SUPERADMIN:
   131→        raise HTTPException(
   132→            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
   133→            detail="Superadmin access required",
   134→        )

   136→
   137→
   138→async def get_current_admin(
   139→    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
   140→) -> User:
   141→    """Require the current user to be an admin or superadmin."""
   142→    if current_user.role not in [UserRole.SUPERADMIN, UserRole.ADMIN]:
   143→        raise HTTPException(

   145→            detail="Admin access required",
   146→        )
   147→    return current_user

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dcambria/scripts/bit-monitoring/backend/app/services/ip_enrichment.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"IP enrichment service using ip-api.com with Redis cache.\n\nip-api.com free tier: 45 req/min.\nCache TTL: 7 days. Multi-worker safe (Redis, no SQLite lock contention).\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport time\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nimport httpx\nimport redis\n\nfrom app.core.config import settings\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass IpEnrichmentService:\n    \"\"\"Enrich IPs with geo/org info via ip-api.com. Redis cache 7 days.\"\"\"\n\n    CACHE_TTL = 7 * 86400  # 7 days\n    RATE_LIMIT_BATCH = 9   # 9 IPs per batch\n    RATE_LIMIT_SLEEP = 12  # 12s between batches → <45 req/min\n\n    def __init__(self):\n        self._redis = redis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL, decode_responses=True)\n\n    def enrich(self, ip: str) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Enrich a single IP. Returns cached result if available.\"\"\"\n        if not ip:\n            return {}\n        cache_key = f\"ip:enrichment:{ip}\"\n        cached = self._redis.get(cache_key)\n        if cached:\n            return json.loads(cached)\n        try:\n            resp = httpx.get(\n                f\"http://ip-api.com/json/{ip}\",\n                params={\"fields\": \"country,countryCode,city,org,as,query\"},\n                timeout=3.0,\n            )\n            data = resp.json()\n            self._redis.setex(cache_key, self.CACHE_TTL, json.dumps(data))\n            return data\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.warning(f\"IP enrichment failed for {ip}: {e}\")\n            return {}\n\n    def enrich_batch(self, ips: list[str]) -> dict[str, dict]:\n        \"\"\"Enrich multiple IPs, respecting rate limits.\n\n        Skips IPs already cached. Batches remaining in groups of 9,\n        sleeping 12s between batches to stay under 45 req/min.\n        \"\"\"\n        results: dict[str, dict] = {}\n\n        # Collect already-cached results\n        new_ips = []\n        for ip in ips:\n            if not ip:\n                continue\n            cache_key = f\"ip:enrichment:{ip}\"\n            cached = self._redis.get(cache_key)\n            if cached:\n                results[ip] = json.loads(cached)\n            else:\n                new_ips.append(ip)\n\n        # Fetch uncached IPs in rate-limited batches\n        for i in range(0, len(new_ips), self.RATE_LIMIT_BATCH):\n            batch = new_ips[i:i + self.RATE_LIMIT_BATCH]\n            for ip in batch:\n                results[ip] = self.enrich(ip)\n            if i + self.RATE_LIMIT_BATCH < len(new_ips):\n                time.sleep(self.RATE_LIMIT_SLEEP)\n\n        # Ensure all input IPs have an entry\n        for ip in ips:\n            if ip and ip not in results:\n                results[ip] = self.enrich(ip)\n\n        return results\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dcambria/scripts/bit-monitoring/backend/app/services/waf_collector.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"WAF log collector — queries CloudWatch Logs Insights for WAF BLOCK events.\n\nSupports both CloudFront WAF (aws-waf-logs-concertacao) and\nALB WAF (aws-waf-logs-amazonia-alb) log groups.\n\nCloudWatch Logs Insights is ASYNC: start_query + poll for completion.\nWatermark cursor: queries only NEW events since last processed timestamp.\n\"\"\"\nimport logging\nimport time\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nfrom app.models.cloud_account import CloudAccount\nfrom app.providers.aws.client import AWSClientFactory\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nCLOUDFRONT_LOG_GROUP = \"aws-waf-logs-concertacao\"\nALB_LOG_GROUP = \"aws-waf-logs-amazonia-alb\"\nPOLL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 60\n\nWAF_QUERY = \"\"\"\nfields @timestamp, action, terminatingRuleId,\n       httpRequest.clientIp, httpRequest.uri, httpRequest.country,\n       httpRequest.httpMethod,\n       httpRequest.headers.0.value as user_agent\n| filter action = 'BLOCK'\n| sort @timestamp asc\n| limit 10000\n\"\"\"\n\n\nclass WafCollectorService:\n    def __init__(self, cloud_account: CloudAccount):\n        self.client_factory = AWSClientFactory(cloud_account)\n        self.region = \"sa-east-1\"\n\n    def query_cloudfront_waf(self, since_timestamp: datetime) -> list[dict]:\n        return self._query_waf_logs(CLOUDFRONT_LOG_GROUP, since_timestamp)\n\n    def query_alb_waf(self, since_timestamp: datetime) -> list[dict]:\n        return self._query_waf_logs(ALB_LOG_GROUP, since_timestamp)\n\n    def _query_waf_logs(self, log_group: str, since_timestamp: datetime) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Start a CloudWatch Logs Insights query and poll until complete.\"\"\"\n        logs_client = self.client_factory.get_client(\"logs\", region=self.region)\n        try:\n            response = logs_client.start_query(\n                logGroupName=log_group,\n                startTime=int(since_timestamp.timestamp()),\n                endTime=int(datetime.utcnow().timestamp()),\n                queryString=WAF_QUERY,\n            )\n            query_id = response[\"queryId\"]\n            logger.info(f\"Started CloudWatch query {query_id} on {log_group}\")\n            return self._poll_query(logs_client, query_id)\n        except logs_client.exceptions.ResourceNotFoundException:\n            logger.warning(f\"Log group {log_group} not found — skipping\")\n            return []\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.error(f\"Failed to query {log_group}: {e}\")\n            return []\n\n    def _poll_query(self, client, query_id: str) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Poll query until Complete, Failed, or timeout.\"\"\"\n        for _ in range(POLL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS):\n            result = client.get_query_results(queryId=query_id)\n            status = result[\"status\"]\n            if status == \"Complete\":\n                logger.info(f\"Query {query_id} complete: {len(result['results'])} rows\")\n                return result[\"results\"]\n            if status in (\"Failed\", \"Cancelled\"):\n                logger.error(f\"Query {query_id} ended with status: {status}\")\n                return []\n            time.sleep(1)\n        logger.warning(f\"Query {query_id} timed out after {POLL_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s\")\n        return []\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dcambria/scripts/bit-monitoring/backend/app/services/security_service.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Security service — aggregation queries for WAF attack data.\"\"\"\nimport logging\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import func, select, text\nfrom sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n\nfrom app.models.security_event import SecurityEvent\nfrom app.schemas.security_event import AttackSummary, SecurityEventRead\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_TOP_N = 20\n\n\nclass SecurityService:\n    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession):\n        self.db = db\n\n    async def get_attack_summary(self, hours: int = 24) -> AttackSummary:\n        since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)\n\n        total = await self.db.scalar(\n            select(func.count(SecurityEvent.id)).where(\n                SecurityEvent.timestamp >= since\n            )\n        )\n\n        top_rules = await self._top_n(\"rule_name\", since)\n        top_countries = await self._top_n(\"country_code\", since)\n        top_ips = await self._top_n(\"ip_address\", since)\n        top_asns = await self._top_n(\"ip_asn\", since)\n        sources = await self._top_n(\"source\", since, limit=10)\n        timeline = await self._timeline(since)\n\n        return AttackSummary(\n            period_hours=hours,\n            total_blocks=total or 0,\n            top_rules=top_rules,\n            top_countries=top_countries,\n            top_ips=top_ips,\n            top_asns=top_asns,\n            timeline=timeline,\n            sources=sources,\n        )\n\n    async def _top_n(self, field: str, since: datetime, limit: int = _TOP_N) -> list[dict]:\n        col = getattr(SecurityEvent, field)\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            select(col, func.count(SecurityEvent.id).label(\"count\"))\n            .where(SecurityEvent.timestamp >= since, col.isnot(None))\n            .group_by(col)\n            .order_by(text(\"count DESC\"))\n            .limit(limit)\n        )\n        return [{\"value\": row[0], \"count\": row[1]} for row in result.all()]\n\n    async def _timeline(self, since: datetime) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Try attack_timeline_1h continuous aggregate first, fallback to raw query.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            result = await self.db.execute(\n                text(\n                    \"SELECT hour, source, block_count \"\n                    \"FROM attack_timeline_1h \"\n                    \"WHERE hour >= :since \"\n                    \"ORDER BY hour ASC\"\n                ),\n                {\"since\": since},\n            )\n            rows = result.all()\n            if rows:\n                return [\n                    {\"hour\": str(r.hour), \"source\": r.source, \"block_count\": r.block_count}\n                    for r in rows\n                ]\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n        # Fallback: raw aggregation\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            select(\n                func.date_trunc(\"hour\", SecurityEvent.timestamp).label(\"hour\"),\n                SecurityEvent.source,\n                func.count(SecurityEvent.id).label(\"block_count\"),\n            )\n            .where(SecurityEvent.timestamp >= since)\n            .group_by(text(\"hour\"), SecurityEvent.source)\n            .order_by(text(\"hour ASC\"))\n        )\n        return [\n            {\"hour\": str(r.hour), \"source\": r.source, \"block_count\": r.block_count}\n            for r in result.all()\n        ]\n\n    async def list_events(\n        self,\n        hours: int = 24,\n        source: Optional[str] = None,\n        rule: Optional[str] = None,\n        page: int = 1,\n        page_size: int = 100,\n    ) -> dict:\n        since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)\n        query = select(SecurityEvent).where(SecurityEvent.timestamp >= since)\n        count_query = select(func.count(SecurityEvent.id)).where(\n            SecurityEvent.timestamp >= since\n        )\n\n        if source:\n            query = query.where(SecurityEvent.source == source)\n            count_query = count_query.where(SecurityEvent.source == source)\n        if rule:\n            query = query.where(SecurityEvent.rule_name == rule)\n            count_query = count_query.where(SecurityEvent.rule_name == rule)\n\n        total = await self.db.scalar(count_query)\n        offset = (page - 1) * page_size\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            query.order_by(SecurityEvent.timestamp.desc())\n            .offset(offset)\n            .limit(page_size)\n        )\n        events = [\n            SecurityEventRead.model_validate(e) for e in result.scalars().all()\n        ]\n        return {\n            \"items\": [e.model_dump() for e in events],\n            \"total\": total or 0,\n            \"page\": page,\n            \"page_size\": page_size,\n        }\n\n    async def get_top_attackers(self, hours: int = 24, limit: int = 20) -> list[dict]:\n        since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)\n        result = await self.db.execute(\n            select(\n                SecurityEvent.ip_address,\n                SecurityEvent.ip_org,\n                SecurityEvent.ip_city,\n                SecurityEvent.ip_asn,\n                SecurityEvent.country_code,\n                func.count(SecurityEvent.id).label(\"count\"),\n            )\n            .where(\n                SecurityEvent.timestamp >= since,\n                SecurityEvent.ip_address.isnot(None),\n            )\n            .group_by(\n                SecurityEvent.ip_address,\n                SecurityEvent.ip_org,\n                SecurityEvent.ip_city,\n                SecurityEvent.ip_asn,\n                SecurityEvent.country_code,\n            )\n            .order_by(text(\"count DESC\"))\n            .limit(limit)\n        )\n        return [\n            {\n                \"ip\": r.ip_address,\n                \"org\": r.ip_org,\n                \"city\": r.ip_city,\n                \"asn\": r.ip_asn,\n                \"country\": r.country_code,\n                \"count\": r.count,\n            }\n            for r in result.all()\n        ]\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dcambria/scripts/bit-monitoring/backend/workers/tasks/waf_collector.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Celery tasks for WAF log collection and attack spike detection.\n\ncollect_waf_events  — runs every 5 min, watermark cursor per source\ndetect_attack_spike — runs every 15 min, Z-score spike detection\n\"\"\"\nimport logging\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n\nfrom sqlalchemy import func, select\nfrom sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as pg_insert\n\nfrom app.core.sync_database import sync_db_session\nfrom app.models.cloud_account import CloudAccount\nfrom app.models.security_event import SecurityEvent\nfrom app.services.ip_enrichment import IpEnrichmentService\nfrom app.services.waf_collector import WafCollectorService\nfrom workers.celery_app import celery_app\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\ndef _get_cloud_account(db) -> CloudAccount:\n    \"\"\"Fetch the active AWS CloudAccount (first active one for now).\"\"\"\n    from app.core.config import settings\n\n    account_id = getattr(settings, \"CONCERTACAO_AWS_ACCOUNT_ID\", None)\n    if account_id:\n        account = db.execute(\n            select(CloudAccount).where(CloudAccount.id == int(account_id))\n        ).scalar_one_or_none()\n        if account:\n            return account\n\n    # Fallback: first active AWS account\n    from app.models.cloud_account import CloudProvider\n    account = db.execute(\n        select(CloudAccount).where(\n            CloudAccount.provider == CloudProvider.AWS,\n            CloudAccount.is_active == True,\n        ).limit(1)\n    ).scalar_one_or_none()\n\n    if not account:\n        raise RuntimeError(\"No active AWS CloudAccount found in database\")\n    return account\n\n\ndef _parse_events(raw_events: list, source: str) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Convert CloudWatch Logs Insights result rows to dicts for upsert.\n\n    CloudWatch Insights format: [{'field': '@timestamp', 'value': '...'}, ...]\n    \"\"\"\n    parsed = []\n    for event in raw_events:\n        fields = {f[\"field\"]: f[\"value\"] for f in event}\n        ts_raw = fields.get(\"@timestamp\", \"\")\n        if not ts_raw:\n            continue\n        try:\n            ts = datetime.fromisoformat(ts_raw.replace(\"Z\", \"+00:00\"))\n        except ValueError:\n            continue\n        parsed.append({\n            \"timestamp\": ts,\n            \"source\": source,\n            \"rule_name\": fields.get(\"terminatingRuleId\"),\n            \"action\": fields.get(\"action\", \"BLOCK\"),\n            \"ip_address\": fields.get(\"httpRequest.clientIp\"),\n            \"user_agent\": fields.get(\"user_agent\"),\n            \"uri\": fields.get(\"httpRequest.uri\"),\n            \"country_code\": fields.get(\"httpRequest.country\"),\n            \"http_method\": fields.get(\"httpRequest.httpMethod\"),\n            \"ip_org\": None,\n            \"ip_city\": None,\n            \"ip_asn\": None,\n        })\n    return parsed\n\n\n@celery_app.task(name=\"workers.tasks.waf_collector.collect_waf_events\")\ndef collect_waf_events():\n    \"\"\"Collect WAF BLOCK events from CloudFront and ALB WAF logs.\n\n    Uses watermark cursor per source (not a fixed 6h window) to avoid\n    re-processing already-seen events. Upserts with on_conflict_do_nothing\n    for safe idempotency.\n    \"\"\"\n    with sync_db_session() as db:\n        try:\n            cloud_account = _get_cloud_account(db)\n        except RuntimeError as e:\n            logger.error(f\"collect_waf_events: {e}\")\n            return\n\n        collector = WafCollectorService(cloud_account)\n        enricher = IpEnrichmentService()\n\n        for source, fetch_fn in [\n            (\"waf_cloudfront\", collector.query_cloudfront_waf),\n            (\"waf_alb\", collector.query_alb_waf),\n        ]:\n            last_ts = db.execute(\n                select(func.max(SecurityEvent.timestamp)).where(\n                    SecurityEvent.source == source\n                )\n            ).scalar()\n\n            since = last_ts if last_ts else (\n                datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=6)\n            )\n\n            logger.info(f\"Fetching {source} events since {since.isoformat()}\")\n            raw = fetch_fn(since_timestamp=since)\n            if not raw:\n                logger.info(f\"No new events for {source}\")\n                continue\n\n            parsed = _parse_events(raw, source)\n            if not parsed:\n                continue\n\n            # Enrich IPs\n            unique_ips = list({e[\"ip_address\"] for e in parsed if e.get(\"ip_address\")})\n            enrichments = enricher.enrich_batch(unique_ips)\n\n            for event in parsed:\n                ip = event.get(\"ip_address\")\n                if ip and ip in enrichments:\n                    geo = enrichments[ip]\n                    event[\"ip_org\"] = geo.get(\"org\")\n                    event[\"ip_city\"] = geo.get(\"city\")\n                    event[\"ip_asn\"] = geo.get(\"as\")\n                    if not event.get(\"country_code\"):\n                        event[\"country_code\"] = geo.get(\"countryCode\")\n\n            stmt = (\n                pg_insert(SecurityEvent)\n                .values(parsed)\n                .on_conflict_do_nothing()\n            )\n            db.execute(stmt)\n            logger.info(f\"Upserted {len(parsed)} {source} events\")\n\n        db.commit()\n\n        # Publish real-time event so frontend refreshes\n        from app.services.realtime import RealtimeService\n        RealtimeService.publish_event_sync(\n            event_type=\"security_event\",\n            data={\"timestamp\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()},\n            target_user_ids=None,\n        )\n\n\n@celery_app.task(name=\"workers.tasks.waf_collector.detect_attack_spike\")\ndef detect_attack_spike():\n    \"\"\"Detect sudden attack spikes: current 15-min window vs. 7-day baseline + 3σ.\n\n    Fires a SECURITY_ATTACK alert via SyncAlertService when spike detected.\n    \"\"\"\n    with sync_db_session() as db:\n        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n        window_start = now - timedelta(minutes=15)\n        baseline_start = now - timedelta(days=7)\n\n        current_count = db.execute(\n            select(func.count(SecurityEvent.id)).where(\n                SecurityEvent.timestamp >= window_start\n            )\n        ).scalar() or 0\n\n        # Baseline: count per 15-min bucket over last 7 days\n        from sqlalchemy import text\n        baseline_rows = db.execute(\n            text(\n                \"SELECT count(*) as cnt FROM security_events \"\n                \"WHERE timestamp BETWEEN :start AND :end \"\n                \"GROUP BY floor(extract(epoch from timestamp) / 900)\"\n            ),\n            {\"start\": baseline_start, \"end\": window_start},\n        ).fetchall()\n\n        if not baseline_rows:\n            logger.info(\"detect_attack_spike: insufficient baseline data\")\n            return\n\n        counts = [row[0] for row in baseline_rows]\n        n = len(counts)\n        mean = sum(counts) / n\n        variance = sum((c - mean) ** 2 for c in counts) / n\n        stddev = variance ** 0.5\n\n        threshold = mean + 3 * stddev\n        logger.info(\n            f\"Attack spike check: current={current_count}, \"\n            f\"baseline mean={mean:.1f}, 3σ threshold={threshold:.1f}\"\n        )\n\n        if current_count > threshold and current_count > 10:\n            # Find top rule and IP in current window\n            top_rule_row = db.execute(\n                select(SecurityEvent.rule_name, func.count(SecurityEvent.id).label(\"cnt\"))\n                .where(SecurityEvent.timestamp >= window_start)\n                .group_by(SecurityEvent.rule_name)\n                .order_by(text(\"cnt DESC\"))\n                .limit(1)\n            ).first()\n            top_ip_row = db.execute(\n                select(SecurityEvent.ip_address, func.count(SecurityEvent.id).label(\"cnt\"))\n                .where(SecurityEvent.timestamp >= window_start)\n                .group_by(SecurityEvent.ip_address)\n                .order_by(text(\"cnt DESC\"))\n                .limit(1)\n            ).first()\n\n            top_rule = top_rule_row[0] if top_rule_row else \"unknown\"\n            top_ip = top_ip_row[0] if top_ip_row else \"unknown\"\n\n            try:\n                from app.models.notification_rule import Severity, TriggerType\n                from app.services.alert_service_sync import SyncAlertService\n\n                alert_service = SyncAlertService(db)\n                alert_service.trigger_alert(\n                    trigger_type=TriggerType.SECURITY_ATTACK,\n                    severity=Severity.CRITICAL,\n                    title=f\"Attack Spike: {current_count} bloqueios em 15min\",\n                    message=(\n                        f\"Detectado pico de ataques: {current_count} bloqueios em 15 min \"\n                        f\"(baseline: {mean:.0f} ± {stddev:.0f}). \"\n                        f\"Regra principal: {top_rule}. IP principal: {top_ip}.\"\n                    ),\n                    resource_type=\"waf\",\n                    resource_id=\"concertacao\",\n                    details={\n                        \"current_count\": current_count,\n                        \"baseline_mean\": round(mean, 1),\n                        \"baseline_stddev\": round(stddev, 1),\n                        \"threshold\": round(threshold, 1),\n                        \"top_rule\": top_rule,\n                        \"top_ip\": top_ip,\n                    },\n                )\n                logger.warning(\n                    f\"ATTACK SPIKE detected: {current_count} blocks in 15min \"\n                    f\"(threshold {threshold:.0f})\"\n                )\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.error(f\"Failed to trigger attack spike alert: {e}\")\n"
}

{

  "content": "\"\"\"Security API endpoints — WAF attack monitoring.\n\nProvides attack summaries, event listings, timeline, and top-attacker data\naggregated from the security_events TimescaleDB hypertable.\n\"\"\"\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query\nfrom sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n\nfrom app.api.deps import get_current_user, get_db\nfrom app.models.user import User\nfrom app.services.security_service import SecurityService\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nrouter = APIRouter()\n\n\n@router.get(\"/summary\")\nasync def get_attack_summary(\n    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=720),\n    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),\n    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n):\n    \"\"\"Return aggregated attack summary for the specified period.\"\"\"\n    service = SecurityService(db)\n    summary = await service.get_attack_summary(hours=hours)\n    return summary\n\n\n@router.get(\"/events\")\nasync def list_events(\n    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=720),\n    source: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n    rule: Optional[str] = Query(None),\n    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),\n    page_size: int = Query(100, ge=1, le=500),\n    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),\n    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n):\n    \"\"\"List security events with optional source/rule filters and pagination.\"\"\"\n    service = SecurityService(db)\n    return await service.list_events(\n        hours=hours,\n        source=source,\n        rule=rule,\n        page=page,\n        page_size=page_size,\n    )\n\n\n@router.get(\"/timeline\")\nasync def get_attack_timeline(\n    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=720),\n    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),\n    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n):\n    \"\"\"Return attack timeline grouped by hour and source.\n\n    Queries the attack_timeline_1h continuous aggregate when available\n    (100x faster), falls back to raw aggregation otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    since_dt = __import__(\"datetime\").datetime.now(\n        __import__(\"datetime\").timezone.utc\n    ) - __import__(\"datetime\").timedelta(hours=hours)\n    service = SecurityService(db)\n    return await service._timeline(since_dt)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/top-attackers\")\nasync def get_top_attackers(\n    hours: int = Query(24, ge=1, le=720),\n    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),\n    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),\n    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),\n):\n    \"\"\"Return top attacking IPs enriched with geo/org data.\"\"\"\n    service = SecurityService(db)\n    return await service.get_top_attackers(hours=hours, limit=limit)\n"
}
