     1→"""add security_events hypertable and attack_timeline_1h aggregate
     2→
     3→Revision ID: r2s3t4u5v6w7
     4→Revises: q1r2s3t4u5v6
     5→Create Date: 2026-03-20
     6→
     7→Creates:
     8→- security_events TimescaleDB hypertable (1-day chunks)
     9→- Compression policy: after 7 days
    10→- Retention policy: 90 days (compliance)
    11→- attack_timeline_1h continuous aggregate (5-min refresh)
    12→- security_attack value in triggertype PostgreSQL enum
    13→"""
    14→from typing import Sequence, Union
    15→
    16→import sqlalchemy as sa
    17→from alembic import op
    18→
    19→revision: str = "r2s3t4u5v6w7"
    20→down_revision: Union[str, None] = "q1r2s3t4u5v6"
    21→branch_labels: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None
    22→depends_on: Union[str, Sequence[str], None] = None
    23→
    24→
    25→def upgrade() -> None:
    26→    # 1. Add security_attack to triggertype enum
    27→    op.execute(
    28→        "ALTER TYPE triggertype ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS 'security_attack'"
    29→    )
    30→
    31→    # 2. Enable TimescaleDB extension (MUST come before create_hypertable)
    32→    op.execute("CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS timescaledb;")
    33→
    34→    # 3. Create the security_events table
    35→    op.create_table(
    36→        "security_events",
    37→        sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
    38→        sa.Column("timestamp", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False),
    39→        sa.Column("source", sa.String(32)),
    40→        sa.Column("rule_name", sa.String(128)),
    41→        sa.Column("action", sa.String(16)),
    42→        sa.Column("country_code", sa.String(2)),
    43→        sa.Column("ip_address", sa.String(45)),
    44→        sa.Column("user_agent", sa.String(512)),
    45→        sa.Column("uri", sa.String(1024)),
    46→        sa.Column("http_method", sa.String(8)),
    47→        sa.Column("status_code", sa.Integer()),
    48→        sa.Column("bot_category", sa.String(64)),
    49→        sa.Column("ip_org", sa.String(128)),
    50→        sa.Column("ip_city", sa.String(64)),
    51→        sa.Column("ip_asn", sa.String(64)),
    52→        sa.Column("threat_score", sa.Float()),
    53→    )
    54→    op.create_index("ix_se_timestamp", "security_events", ["timestamp"])
    55→    op.create_index("ix_se_source_ts", "security_events", ["source", "timestamp"])
    56→    op.create_index("ix_se_ip_ts", "security_events", ["ip_address", "timestamp"])
    57→
    58→    # 4. Convert to TimescaleDB hypertable (1-day chunks)
    59→    op.execute(
    60→        """
    61→        SELECT create_hypertable(
    62→            'security_events', 'timestamp',
    63→            chunk_time_interval => INTERVAL '1 day',
    64→            if_not_exists => TRUE
    65→        )
    66→        """
    67→    )
    68→
    69→    # 5. Auto-compression after 7 days (~60% storage savings)
    70→    op.execute(
    71→        """
    72→        SELECT add_compression_policy(
    73→            'security_events',
    74→            INTERVAL '7 days',
    75→            if_not_exists => true
    76→        )
    77→        """
    78→    )
    79→
    80→    # 6. Retention: 90 days for compliance
    81→    op.execute(
    82→        """
    83→        SELECT add_retention_policy(
    84→            'security_events',
    85→            INTERVAL '90 days',
    86→            if_not_exists => true
    87→        )
    88→        """
    89→    )
    90→
    91→    # 7. Continuous aggregate for timeline queries (frontend charts)
    92→    try:
    93→        op.execute(
    94→            """
    95→            CREATE MATERIALIZED VIEW IF NOT EXISTS attack_timeline_1h
    96→            WITH (timescaledb.continuous) AS
    97→            SELECT
    98→                time_bucket('1 hour', timestamp) AS hour,
    99→                source,
   100→                action,
   101→                count(*) AS block_count
   102→            FROM security_events
   103→            GROUP BY hour, source, action;
   104→            """
   105→        )
   106→        op.execute(
   107→            """
   108→            SELECT add_continuous_aggregate_policy(
   109→                'attack_timeline_1h',
   110→                start_offset  => INTERVAL '3 hours',
   111→                end_offset    => INTERVAL '0 seconds',
   112→                schedule_interval => INTERVAL '5 minutes',
   113→                if_not_exists => true
   114→            )
   115→            """
   116→        )
   117→    except Exception:
   118→        # Fallback: regular view if continuous aggregates not available
   119→        op.execute(
   120→            """
   121→            CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW attack_timeline_1h AS
   122→            SELECT
   123→                date_trunc('hour', timestamp) AS hour,
   124→                source,
   125→                action,
   126→                count(*) AS block_count
   127→            FROM security_events
   128→            GROUP BY hour, source, action;
   129→            """
   130→        )
   131→
   132→
   133→def downgrade() -> None:
   134→    # Remove TimescaleDB policies before dropping table (avoid orphaned jobs)
   135→    for stmt in [
   136→        "SELECT remove_continuous_aggregate_policy('attack_timeline_1h', if_exists => true)",
   137→        "SELECT remove_retention_policy('security_events', if_exists => true)",
   138→        "SELECT remove_compression_policy('security_events', if_exists => true)",
   139→    ]:
   140→        try:
   141→            op.execute(stmt)
   142→        except Exception:
   143→            pass
   144→
   145→    op.execute("DROP MATERIALIZED VIEW IF EXISTS attack_timeline_1h CASCADE")
   146→    op.execute("DROP VIEW IF EXISTS attack_timeline_1h CASCADE")
   147→    op.drop_table("security_events")

Create the security_events table\n    op.create_table(\n        \"security_events\",\n        # BigInteger prevents int32 overflow at ~2B rows\n        sa.Column(\"id\", sa.BigInteger(), primary_key=True),\n        sa.Column(\"timestamp\", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=False),\n        sa.Column(\"source\", sa.String(32)),\n        sa.Column(\"rule_name\", sa.String(128)),\n        sa.Column(\"action\", sa.String(16)),\n        sa.Column(\"country_code\", sa.String(2)),\n        sa.Column(\"ip_address\", sa.String(45)),\n        sa.Column(\"user_agent\", sa.String(512)),\n        sa.Column(\"uri\", sa.String(1024)),\n        sa.Column(\"http_method\", sa.String(8)),\n        sa.Column(\"status_code\", sa.BigInteger()),\n        sa.Column(\"bot_category\", sa.String(64)),\n        sa.Column(\"ip_org\", sa.String(128)),\n        sa.Column(\"ip_city\", sa.String(64)),\n        sa.Column(\"ip_asn\", sa.String(64)),\n        sa.Column(\"threat_score\", sa.Float()),\n        # Unique constraint required by on_conflict_do_nothing deduplication\n        sa.UniqueConstraint(\n            \"source\", \"timestamp\", \"ip_address\", \"rule_name\",\n            name=\"uq_se_source_ts_ip_rule\",\n        ),\n    )"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/dcambria/scripts/bit-monitoring/frontend/src/app/security/components/_AttackTimelineChart.tsx",
  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport {\n  BarChart,\n  Bar,\n  XAxis,\n  YAxis,\n  Tooltip,\n  Legend,\n  ResponsiveContainer,\n} from 'recharts'\n\ninterface TimelineRow {\n  hour: string\n  source: string\n  block_count: number\n}\n\ninterface Props {\n  data: TimelineRow[] | undefined\n}\n\nexport default function AttackTimelineChart({ data }: Props) {\n  // Pivot: group by hour, columns = sources\n  const byHour: Record<string, Record<string, number>> = {}\n  for (const row of data ?? []) {\n    // Extract hour portion: \"2026-03-20T14\" → \"14:00\"\n    const h = row.hour.slice(0, 13)\n    if (!byHour[h]) byHour[h] = {}\n    byHour[h][row.source] = (byHour[h][row.source] ?? 0) + row.block_count\n  }\n\n  const chartData = Object.entries(byHour)\n    .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))\n    .map(([hour, counts]) => ({\n      hour: hour.length >= 13 ? hour.slice(11) + ':00' : hour,\n      ...counts,\n    }))\n\n  if (chartData.length === 0) {\n    return <p className=\"text-bit-muted text-sm\">Nenhum dado disponível.</p>\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <ResponsiveContainer width=\"100%\" height={220}>\n      <BarChart data={chartData}>\n        <XAxis dataKey=\"hour\" tick={{ fill: '#9ca3af', fontSize: 11 }} />\n        <YAxis tick={{ fill: '#9ca3af', fontSize: 11 }} />\n        <Tooltip\n          contentStyle={{ background: '#1e1e2e', border: '1px solid #334155', color: '#e2e8f0' }}\n        />\n        <Legend />\n        <Bar dataKey=\"waf_cloudfront\" name=\"CloudFront WAF\" fill=\"#f97316\" stackId=\"a\" />\n        <Bar dataKey=\"waf_alb\" name=\"ALB WAF\" fill=\"#3b82f6\" stackId=\"a\" />\n      </BarChart>\n    </ResponsiveContainer>\n  )\n}\n"
}

  "new_string": "  local query_id\n  local aws_stderr_file=\"${TMP_DIR}/aws_stderr_$$\"\n  # Capture stderr separately so query_id only contains the actual ID\n  if ! query_id=$(aws logs start-query \\\n    --log-group-name \"${log_group}\" \\\n    --start-time \"${START_TS}\" \\\n    --end-time \"${END_TS}\" \\\n    --query-string \"${query_string}\" \\\n    --region \"${region}\" \\\n    --profile \"${AWS_PROFILE}\" \\\n    --output text --query 'queryId' 2>\"${aws_stderr_file}\"); then\n    local aws_err\n    aws_err=$(cat \"${aws_stderr_file}\" 2>/dev/null || true)\n    log_warn \"Query falhou para ${log_group} (${region}): ${aws_err}\"\n    echo \"[]\" > \"${out_file}\"\n    return 0\n  fi\n\n  if [[ -z \"${query_id}\" ]]; then\n    log_warn \"Query retornou ID vazio para ${log_group} (${region})\"\n    echo \"[]\" > \"${out_file}\"\n    return 0\n  fi"
}

O push via SSH está bloqueado neste contexto. Preparei o PR completo — você precisa fazer o push manualmente:

```bash
cd ~/scripts/bit-monitoring
git push -u origin feat/security-attack-monitoring
gh pr create \
  --title "feat: módulo Attack Monitoring — security_events (WAF CloudFront + ALB)" \
  --body "$(cat <<'EOF'
## Summary

- **SecurityEvent** TimescaleDB hypertable com BigInteger PK, UniqueConstraint para deduplicação via `on_conflict_do_nothing`
- **WafCollectorService** consulta CloudWatch Logs Insights (async: `start_query` + polling) para CloudFront WAF e ALB WAF
- **IpEnrichmentService** enriquece IPs via ip-api.com com Redis cache 7 dias (rate-limited, multi-worker safe)
- **Celery tasks**: `collect_waf_events` (5min, watermark cursor +1s) + `detect_attack_spike` (15min, Z-score amostral 3σ)
- **4 endpoints FastAPI**: `/security/summary`, `/events`, `/timeline`, `/top-attackers`
- **Dashboard `/security`**: KPI cards, BarChart empilhado, PieChart de regras, tabela top atacantes, eventos paginados com reset de página ao trocar período
- **Alembic migration**: hypertable + compressão 7d + retenção 90d + continuous aggregate `attack_timeline_1h`

## Pré-requisitos para ativar

1. **Passo 0 (manual)**: Habilitar logging WAF ALB → `aws-waf-logs-amazonia-alb` (comandos no plano)
2. `alembic upgrade head`
3. Definir `CONCERTACAO_AWS_ACCOUNT_ID=<id>` no `.env`
4. Reiniciar workers Celery

## Test plan

- [ ] `alembic upgrade head` aplica sem erro (hypertable + continuous aggregate)
- [ ] `celery inspect active` → tasks `collect_waf_events` e `detect_attack_spike` registradas
- [ ] `GET /api/v1/security/summary?hours=24` retorna JSON com `total_blocks`
- [ ] Frontend `/security` carrega, Sidebar visível, charts renderizam
- [ ] Trocar período no seletor → `RecentEventsTable` volta para página 1
- [ ] DevTools Network → WebSocket → frame `security_event` recebido após coleta
- [ ] Spike test → alerta Slack via `SyncAlertService.trigger_alert(TriggerType.SECURITY_ATTACK)`

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)
EOF
)"
```

---

## Resumo

**3 agentes revisaram o código** e encontraram 13 problemas críticos/avisos entre backend, frontend e script. Todos foram corrigidos:

### Críticos corrigidos
| # | Problema | Correção |
|---|---------|---------|
| C-01 | `on_conflict_do_nothing` sem constraint — duplicatas | `UniqueConstraint` + `index_elements` |
| C-02 | Watermark rebusca evento da fronteira | `since = last_ts + timedelta(seconds=1)` |
| C-03 | Double-commit + RealtimeService dentro do `with` | Removido commit manual; publish movido para fora do `with` |
| C-04 | `__import__("datetime")` inline no endpoint | `from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone` |
| C-05 | `except Exception` silencioso na migration | `logger.warning(f"... ({e})")` explícito |
| C-FE1 | `React.ReactNode` sem import | `import type { ReactNode } from 'react'` |
| C-FE2 | Page reset faltando ao trocar período | `useEffect(() => setPage(1), [hours])` |
| C-FE3 | Recharts com SSR → `ReferenceError: window` | `dynamic(() => import('./_Chart'), { ssr: false })` |
| C-SH1-4 | `mktemp`, validação `--days`, datas macOS, `query_id` com stderr | Todos corrigidos |

### Avisos corrigidos
`Integer→BigInteger`, `utcnow()→timezone.utc`, variância amostral, `order_by(count_col.desc())`, WebSocket `useCallback`, paginação robusta, `more` fallback no help.

**O commit está em `feat/security-attack-monitoring` no bit-monitoring** aguardando push. O script `waf-attack-report.sh` foi commitado no server-tools v2.
